# SGW_Menfa_Complete_Dev_Master_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Menfa_Complete_Dev_Master_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Workbook | SGW Men'Fa — Complete Dev Master v1 |
| Strict scope | Men'Fa local runtime only. Two canonical client worlds are preserved separately: Menfa_Light World78 for SGU, Menfa_Dark World77 for Praxis. Beta/Omega/High Council/Harset are OFFWORLD handoffs only. |
| SGU route | Beta Site clue → Omega leadership → Menfa_Light. Core Light storyline pursues cure for Nine Peaks plague and ends Mission1170 at Omega/Heimdall. |
| Praxis route | Harset/Praxis leadership → Menfa_Dark. Core Dark storyline pursues the Mouth of Ptah/Book of Gates and ends Mission1102 at Ba'al on Harset. |
| Faction coverage | All3 SGU classes use Light; all3 Praxis races use Dark. Explicit local faction side quests: SGU Human1428, Goa'uld1220, Loyalist Jaffa1327. OP-Core-specific local side mission not proven. Mission1143 remains unresolved. |
| Maps | Excellent recovery:272 production files for Dark and272 for Light, each with main UMAP +270 streaming cells + MapData. Dark all in Maps005; Light spans Maps005 plus15 cells in Maps006. |
| Kismet | 114 Men'Fa references: both Stargates plus extensive ring-transporter sequences. One Light-associated record references a Dark script path and is flagged for audit. |
| Dialogues | Relevant Men'Fa mission topics yield311 recovered dialogue screen rows and167 interaction rows; no '<Input text here>' placeholders detected. |
| Can we build it? | YES. Men'Fa is one of the strongest reconstruction candidates: both original world variants, gates, minimaps, Kismet, mission logic, dialogs, items and interaction effects survive. It is not yet fully 1:1 because server mission grants, NPC/enemy spawn tables, exact task→actor bindings and audio-event mapping are still missing. |
| Evidence labels | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED; RECONSTRUCTION / INFERENCE; PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING; OUT OF SCOPE / LEGACY suffixes where appropriate. |
| Authority rule | Raw cooked client/runtime evidence outranks secondary spreadsheets/progression. Never join duplicate missions by title alone; use MissionID/interaction set/variant context. |

_11 data rows, 2 columns._
